33. She writes in her workbook:
    - Clash line: the line she saw under "Short code"
    - Short code saved: TCDDA2
    - Toast: the exact text of the message that popped up after "Create workshop"
    - Group: the group in the list where the workshop landed

She should see:

- The toast "Workshop created." A toast is the small message that pops up after she clicks a button.
- The screen switches to the "Published Links" tab, with the new workshop selected under "Drafts · N awaiting publication". The count includes the practice clash draft.
- The workshop card has a gold "Draft" pill. The pill is the small coloured label on the card, and gold means private. The card also shows the price "R 450,00" with a line "Members R 350,00", and the date line "14 Nov 2026, 08:30 to 14 Nov 2026, 12:30 SAST".
- The header reads "0 seats paid · 0 bookings incomplete · 0 bookings total". Below it: "Seats paid 0", "Bookings incomplete 0", "Capacity 0/40" and "Price".
- The "Application link" box, which will hold the public booking link, reads "Publish this workshop to activate the application link."
- No email is sent at this step.

If she gets stuck (give one hint at a time):

- While she types the first details: "Before you leave the first section, look at the line under the short code." Then: "Think about how you move from one box to the next."
- At the price and venue: "Is every line on your sheet a fact yet, or still a plan?" Then: "Think about what a member reading the booking page would believe."
- At the certificate: "What will be printed on each teacher's certificate?" Then: "Check the screen a member will see before you save."
- When Deon asks for the link: "Before you answer, look at what the screen says about the link." Then: "Decide what you can promise today, and say when."

If it goes wrong:

- The line under "Short code" reads "Suggested from the title. Available" instead of the clash message: the practice draft "TRAINING - Short Code Clash - delete after" is missing. Let her save, and cover the clash message in the debrief.
- Red banner "Workshop could not be created. Title and description are required. Your entries are still here; review them and retry.": usually Enter was pressed in the title box. Her entries are kept. She fills the missing box and clicks "Create workshop" again.
- "Enter the CPTD points, or tick No CPTD points for this workshop.": the points choice is required. She takes the points from her sheet, or ticks the no-points box.
- "Use a valid date range where the end is after the start.": she fixes the end time.
- "Member price cannot be more than the standard (non-member) price.": the two prices are the wrong way round.
- "Capacity is required. Every workshop needs a maximum number of attendees, or tick Unlimited below.": she types the number of seats or ticks Unlimited.
- "A in person workshop needs a venue - say where people should go, or set the mode to Venue to be confirmed." (it says "hybrid (in person and online)" for a hybrid workshop): she types the venue or clicks "To be confirmed".
- "Could not check now. You can still save; we check again when you save." under "Short code": she may save. If the save is refused, she sees the toast "Workshop not saved. Short code: ..." and red text under the box. She picks another code or clicks the "Use ..." button.
- "Workshop created, but some documents did not save. Open this workshop's Documents section to upload the remaining files.": the workshop is saved. She uploads the files again from "Workshop files".

Cannot be undone: The workshop "TRAINING - Classroom Discipline - delete after" now exists. It can only be removed with "Delete workshop" while nobody has paid for it, and that cannot be undone either. Its public link is built from the title and never changes. The short code TCDDA2 is now taken. No email is sent.

If you are running late: Never drop this block. Shorten the certificate part by letting the points message under the box teach it, and keep the "Preview" check brief.

Debrief:

1. "Which line on your planning sheet changed what you typed into the form? What would the booking page have said if you had missed it?"
2. "Where did the form surprise you, and what did you do next?"
3. "What exactly did you tell Deon about the link, and what on the screen backed it up? In your own words: when is a workshop live, and how can you tell from the screen?"

How did she do?

- Draft created with the sheet's facts. Done well when she sees the toast "Workshop created." and a gold "Draft" card under "Drafts" with the right title, date line, "R 450,00" plus "Members R 350,00" and "Capacity 0/40". Partly when the draft saved but one fact was wrong or missing (for example capacity, member price or end time) and she noticed only when prompted. Missed when no draft saved, or it saved with a title other than "TRAINING - Classroom Discipline - delete after".
- Venue status checked and shown honestly. Done well when she read the venue note on her sheet and chose "To be confirmed", the hint read "Pages and emails say the venue is still being arranged.", and she could say the booking page will show "Venue to be confirmed - we will email you as soon as it is arranged". Partly when she saw the note but typed a guessed venue with "(TBC)", or chose the chip without being able to say why. Missed when she never checked and the workshop shows an unbooked venue as fact.
- Certificate facts entered from the sheet. Done well when she entered the points, SACE number and facilitator from her sheet before saving, and checked the "Certificate preview". Partly when she entered them only after the system refused to save, or skipped the preview. Missed when she ticked "No CPTD points for this workshop" or left facts out.
- Told the colleague the truth about draft versus live. Done well when she said plainly it is a draft and not public yet, and agreed a clear publish moment with Deon. Partly when she said it is a draft but left the next step vague. Missed when she promised a live link today without publishing, or said the link was already live.

09:33 · The venue is confirmed: she updates the details, publishes, shares the link and adds the poster (9 min)

Goal: She learns that a workshop only becomes public when she clicks "Publish workshop", and that she can change the details of a draft without touching its title. She realises that the link is for WhatsApp and email, and the booking QR code is for a printed poster. She now knows that the system cannot email a link to all schools, that she cannot make a discount code herself, and that removing a file cannot be undone.

Who drives: Her screen. You play a colleague on the phone for the middle part (steps 9 to 33).

Before you start:

- The workshop "TRAINING - Classroom Discipline - delete after" is selected in the "Published Links" tab, under "Drafts". The practice clash draft sits in "Drafts" too, so check she has the right card.
- She is still signed in as the Workshop creator (she can create, edit and publish workshops). She still has no Finance tick, so she cannot do money actions such as discount codes.
- On her desktop:
  - Poster: the prepared TRAINING poster picture
  - Agenda: the prepared TRAINING agenda PDF
  - Second poster: the second prepared TRAINING poster (only for the optional poster swap)
- A phone to scan the downloaded QR code (optional).
- Her workbook, to write down the toast text, the group the workshop moves to and the name of the QR file.
- Say the "cannot be undone" warning below now, before the scene starts.
- Read her the learner card in your coach voice: "The workshop you set up is now confirmed, and a manager needs something to share today. Your job is to make it bookable, give him the right things to share, and be clear about what the system will and will not do for him. You are the Workshop creator. You do not have the Finance tick."

You play: Mr Deon Pretorius, NAPTOSA WC Professional Development manager. Practical, a little impatient, used to doing things himself. He talks in short sentences and thinks about what he can print and send. He phoned on Friday for the link and heard it was a draft. Now he wants to send it on the principals' WhatsApp group and print an A3 poster for the district office tomorrow. The call starts at step 9, after she has updated the details.

Open with: "Hi, Deon here again. I hear the venue is confirmed. I want to send the discipline workshop on the principals' WhatsApp group now and I am printing an A3 poster for the district office tomorrow. Can you send me the link, and something I can stick on the poster so they can scan it?"

Keep these to yourself until she asks:

- Deon would like a discount for principals. Say this only if she asks whether there is anything else he needs for the circular or the poster.
- Deon assumes the system holds the Metro North school addresses and can email them. You raise this yourself, as a request.
- Deon plans to print the check-in QR code on the same poster to save a trip. You raise this yourself, last.

How Deon behaves:

- He never gives a real phone number, ID, PERSAL or NAP number.
- He never asks anyone to pay, and never mentions card or bank details.
- He accepts "there is no bulk send" and the hand-off to Finance once she says it clearly. He pushes back once at most.
- He never claims a button or email exists that is not on the screen.

What happens:

First: the venue is confirmed, so she updates the details

1. You say: "It is now Monday. The Parow Teachers' Centre booking is confirmed."
2. She clicks "Edit details" in the header of the selected workshop. The form opens with the heading "Edit Workshop" and a "Cancel edit" chip.
3. Under "How it runs" she clicks "In person".
   - How it runs: changes from To be confirmed to In person
4. She types the venue on two lines. Enter makes a new line in this box.
   - Venue and address: changes from empty to Parow Teachers' Centre, on two lines
5. She changes the number of seats:
   - Capacity: changes from 40 to 60
6. Everything else stays the same.
   - Title: stays "TRAINING - Classroom Discipline - delete after". She does not touch it.
7. She clicks "Save changes".
8. She sees the toast "Workshop updated." A toast is the small message that pops up after she clicks a button. The workshop is still under "Drafts" and still selected.

Part one of the call: get it live

9. You say the opening line above.
10. She looks at the link box. The "Application link" box holds the public booking link. It reads "Publish this workshop to activate the application link." "Copy link", "Open link" and "Booking link QR" are all greyed out. The booking QR is a picture of a code. Anyone who scans it with a phone lands on the same booking page as the link. For now it says "Publish first to get a booking QR."
11. If she tries "Copy link" while it is still a draft, let her find out it does not work. Say as the narrator: "Deon waits on the line."
12. She clicks "Publish workshop" in the header of the selected workshop. A draft has no confirm box, so it publishes straight away.
13. She reads the toast out loud: "Workshop link is active. Registrations are open."
14. She shows that the card has moved to "Current & upcoming" and has a green "Published" pill. The pill is the small coloured label on the card, and green means live.

Part two of the call: the link and the poster QR

15. You say: "Right. So what do I put on WhatsApp, and what goes on the poster?"
16. She clicks once in the "Application link" box. One click selects the whole link. She cannot type in this box.
17. She clicks "Copy link". The toast reads "Application link copied."
18. She clicks "Open link". The public booking page opens in a new tab with the heading "Register for this workshop". She reads it the way a member would, then closes the tab.
19. She clicks "Booking link QR" and reads the box that opens.
20. She clicks "Download QR (PNG)". A picture file whose name starts with booking-qr- lands in her Downloads folder.
21. She tells Deon the link is for WhatsApp and the QR is for the poster.
22. She reads the header out loud:
    - Header line: 0 seats paid · 0 bookings incomplete · 0 bookings total
    - Capacity: 0/60
23. She opens the Events page on the NAPTOSA WC website, finds the workshop card and clicks "Register". The same booking page opens in a new tab.

Watch for this: the booking page shows her poster if she has uploaded one, or the default NAPTOSA poster if not. Both are correct. Do not let her paste the link into a real WhatsApp group.

Part three of the call: "can the system send it for me?"

24. You say: "Actually, do not bother with the link. Just email it from the system to all the Metro North schools, you have their addresses on the member list, right?"
25. She tells Deon the system has no button to send to all schools. It only emails people after they book.
26. She offers him the copied link or the QR code to send himself.
27. She asks Deon whether there is anything else he needs.
28. You tell her, only if she asked, that you would like a discount for the principals.
29. She tells Deon that discount codes are set up by Finance and that she will pass the details on. She does not promise a code or an amount.

Watch for this: a good hand-off names Finance. In the Western Cape that means the super admins. Nobody in the province has the Finance tick, and there is no auditor. Say it to her in words in the debrief: if she looked down the Finance column on the staff list, she would find no tick for anyone in the Western Cape. There is no auditor row to show her. If she says she will create the coupon herself, stay in character and accept it. The debrief catches it.

Part four of the call (optional if time is short): two QR codes on one poster

30. You say: "Okay, then also give me the check-in QR now so I can print both on the same poster. Saves me a trip."
31. She says no to putting the check-in QR on the public poster.
32. She explains why. The check-in QR ("Check-in link & QR") is for the venue door on the day. Before the day, the check-in page says "Check-in isn't open today". The booking QR is the one for the poster.
    She may open "Check-in link & QR" to show it exists. That is fine. Check-in is taught properly later in the day, so a plain explanation is enough here.
33. You end the call and switch to your coach voice. Give one thing she did well and one thing to improve.

Last: the poster and the agenda

34. In "Published Links", with the workshop selected, she opens "Registrations, coupons and documents" and finds "Workshop files".
    "Workshop files" is a staff shelf for agendas and notes. Staff can download from it. The creator can upload to it and remove from it. Nothing on it is ever emailed to attendees.
35. She reads its notes. The first picture uploaded is the public poster while the workshop is published. Other files are not sent to attendees. A second note reads: "The poster shows on the booking page only. To take a workshop off the website’s Events page, use Close registrations."
36. You say: "Each file here has a Remove button, but a removal cannot be undone. So upload only the prepared TRAINING poster and agenda. Never a test picture."
37. Under "Add workshop files" she chooses two files:
    - Poster: the prepared TRAINING poster picture (PNG, JPG or WebP)
    - Agenda: the prepared TRAINING agenda PDF
38. She clicks "Upload 2 documents". She uploads them once only. She does not add a second picture here. It would only become a staff reference.
39. She reads the list and clicks "Download" on the agenda, to prove staff can fetch it. She sees "Remove" next to each file and does not click it.
40. (Optional if time is short) To swap the poster, she clicks "Edit details" in the header, opens "4. Documents & Media" and reads "Saved with this workshop".
41. (Optional) She drops the second prepared TRAINING poster on the box marked "Upload a workshop picture", or clicks "Browse files". The old poster now reads "Public poster · replaced when you save changes".
42. (Optional) She can click "Preview" to see the card with the new picture. Then she clicks "Save changes".
43. (Optional) Back in "Published Links" she opens "Workshop files" and checks that her new picture is the one tagged "Public poster · shown on the booking page".
44. She reads the header:
    - Header line: N seats paid · N bookings incomplete · N bookings total
    - Capacity: for example 0/60
    - Price: the price box next to it
45. She clicks "Open link" to see the poster on the booking page.
46. You say: "Keep this workshop open. The bookings in the next part go on it. It would only close by itself at 00:05 the night after its date in November, so we close it ourselves in the last clean-up block of the day."

She should see:

- After "Save changes" on the details: the toast "Workshop updated."
- After "Publish workshop": the toast "Workshop link is active. Registrations are open." The card moves to "Current & upcoming" with a green "Published" pill, and the pill in the header also reads "Published".
- After "Copy link": the toast "Application link copied."
- The booking page in a new tab: the caption "NAPTOSA Workshop", the heading "Register for this workshop", the place line, the fee, and "NAPTOSA members R…" because there is a member rate. The place line shows the venue. (While a venue is still "To be confirmed" it shows "Venue to be confirmed - we will email you as soon as it is arranged".) The picture is her poster, or the default NAPTOSA poster if none is uploaded yet.
- In the "Booking link QR" box: "Put this QR code in an email or on a poster for <title>. Scanning it opens the public booking page, the same page as the link below."
- After "Download QR (PNG)": a picture file whose name starts with booking-qr- in Downloads. If she clicks "Copy QR image" instead, the toast reads "QR code copied. Paste it straight into your email."
- On the website Events page: the workshop card with a "Register" button and no poster picture. If the paid seats run out, it shows the word "Full" with no link instead.
- After "Upload 2 documents": the toast "Documents added to the workshop." The poster is listed as "Public poster · shown on the booking page" and the agenda as "Staff reference · download here", each with its size in KB, and a "Remove" button beside "Download".
- After the optional poster swap and "Save changes": the toast "Workshop updated." and she is back in "Published Links" on the same workshop. The new poster shows on the booking page whole, never cropped.
- At the end of the block the workshop is still under "Current & upcoming" with a green "Published" pill.

If she gets stuck (give one hint at a time):

- At "get it live": "Look at the link box first. Can anything be shared yet?" Then: "Check where the workshop sits in the list after you act."
- At the link and the poster: "Two different things to share, for two different places." Then: "Before you send it, look at it the way a principal would."
- At "can the system send it?": "Before you answer, picture the screen. Is there a button for this?" Then: "Decide whose job each part of his request is."
- At the two QR codes: "Think about who scans a poster, and when." Then: "What would a teacher see if she scanned it next week?"

If it goes wrong:

- "Workshop changes could not be saved. …": her entries are still on screen. She reads the reason, fixes that box and clicks "Save changes" again.
- Toast "The workshop dates have passed. Review the dates or close registrations before sharing its link.": the workshop lands in "Needs closure", the group for published workshops whose dates have passed, and Copy and Open are greyed out. She clicks "Edit details", fixes the dates, clicks "Save changes", then shares.
- A red toast that stays on the screen, for example "Status update failed.": she clicks Refresh (the circular arrow, "Refresh workshops") and tries "Publish workshop" once more. If it fails again, she tells the super admin and reads them the toast.
- "Clipboard access was blocked. Open the workshop, select its Application link field and copy it manually.": she clicks the link box, then copies with Ctrl+C.
- "This browser can't copy images, so the QR code was downloaded instead. Attach the file to your email.": she uses the downloaded picture as an attachment.
- "Copy failed. Select the link and copy it by hand." in the QR box: she selects the link text and copies it with Ctrl+C.
- The workshop is not on the Events page: check that it is a Western Cape workshop, published, not closed and not yet ended. Workshops from other provinces are only reachable by link.
- "Will not upload: larger than 25 MB." or "Will not upload: not a supported file type." on a file in the Edit upload box: she clicks the X ("Remove <file>") and chooses a smaller or supported file.
- "Documents could not be added. <reason>" under "Workshop files": "Add workshop files" does not check the size before uploading, so a file that is too big fails here. She shrinks it and uploads again.
- "Workshop updated and the new picture saved, but the previous poster could not be removed, so it is still the public poster. Check Workshop files under Published Links.": she does not upload again blindly. She opens "Workshop files", sees which picture is tagged "Public poster", and tries "Edit details" once more.
- After "Save changes", an older picture is tagged "Public poster" instead of the new one: a second picture was uploaded earlier through "Workshop files". The poster is always the earliest picture still on file. She stops re-uploading. She clicks "Remove" on the older picture. The box reads "Remove the public poster “{file name}”?" and names the picture that becomes the poster next. She clicks "Remove file" and checks the tags again.
- She uploaded a second picture through "Workshop files" and the poster did not change: a later picture is listed as "Staff reference". She swaps the poster through "Edit details" instead, then checks "Workshop files" as above.
- The wrong agenda was uploaded: she clicks "Remove" next to it. The box reads "Remove “{file name}”?" and "Staff will no longer be able to download it. This cannot be undone." She checks the file name, clicks "Remove file" and sees the toast "“{file name}” removed." Then she uploads the right file with a clear name, for example "Agenda FINAL".

Cannot be undone: The workshop goes public. The link works for anyone. The workshop is listed on the website Events page until its end time passes or you close it. The booking link keeps taking bookings until someone clicks "Close registrations", or until the nightly run closes it at 00:05 after its end date. The uploaded poster and agenda stay until someone removes them, and a removal cannot be undone. The optional poster swap removes the old poster for good.

If you are running late: Skip the optional poster swap (steps 40 to 43) and the part about two QR codes on one poster (steps 30 to 32). Check-in is taught later in the day. Never paste the link into a real group.

Debrief:

1. "What did the screen tell you before you published, and what changed straight after? When do Copy link and Open link work, and when are they greyed out?"
2. "Deon asked for three things the system does not do the way he hoped. Which was yours to do, which was Finance's, and which was a no?"
3. "Why does the booking QR go on the poster and not the check-in QR?"

How did she do?

- Workshop published and checked on screen. Done well when she clicked "Publish workshop", read the toast "Workshop link is active. Registrations are open." and showed the card under "Current & upcoming" with a green "Published" pill. Partly when it was published but she did not check the toast or the group. Missed when it was not published, or she tried to share a draft link.
- The right thing shared to the right place. Done well when the link was copied (toast seen), the booking page was opened and checked, and the booking QR was downloaded, with the link matched to WhatsApp and the QR to the poster. Partly when she shared only one of the two, or skipped checking the booking page. Missed when neither was shared, or she gave the check-in QR for the poster.
- Honest about bulk email and the hand-off to Finance. Done well when she said plainly there is no bulk send, and named Finance for the discount without promising a code. Partly when she said no to the bulk send but was vague about who handles the discount. Missed when she promised a bulk email, or said she would create the discount herself.
- Check-in QR kept off the public poster. Done well when she said no and gave the reason: it is for the door on the day, and it is not open before then. Partly when she said no without a reason, or hesitated and said she would think about it. Missed when she gave him the check-in QR for the poster.

09:42 · Quick self-check quiz (3 min)

Goal: She realises that she can answer real situations from this part without looking at the screen. She now knows that "Workshop created." does not mean the link is live.

Who drives: Her. You read each situation out loud and she answers in her own words.

What happens:

1. You always ask the first question below.
2. If there is time, you pick one or two more.
3. The rest go into her workbook for later.

The questions, with the right answer:

1. Always ask this one. You have just clicked "Create workshop" and the toast (the small message that pops up after a click) says "Workshop created." A colleague asks for the booking link to put on WhatsApp right now. What is true, and what do you do?
   - Right answer: It is a draft. Open "Published Links" > "Drafts", click "Publish workshop", then "Copy link".
   - Why: A new workshop always lands in "Drafts". Copy and Open only work once it is published, and only before its end date passes.
   - Wrong answers she may give: "The link is already live" (no, the box says "Publish this workshop to activate the application link."). "Ask the super admin to switch it on" (no need, as Workshop creator she publishes it herself). "Links go live once the first person pays" (no, payment has nothing to do with it).
2. The presenter emails a nicer poster two days after the workshop was published. How do you swap the poster?
   - Right answer: "Edit details" > "4. Documents & Media" > choose the new picture > "Save changes".
   - Why: The old poster is removed when she saves. A picture added under "Workshop files" (the staff shelf for agendas and notes) only becomes a "Staff reference" while the old poster is still on file, and it is not a Finance job. Afterwards she checks in "Workshop files" that the new picture is tagged "Public poster · shown on the booking page".
3. You uploaded the wrong agenda PDF to "Workshop files". The right one is on your desktop. What do you do?
   - Right answer: Click "Remove" next to the wrong agenda, check the file name, click "Remove file", then upload the right one with a clear name such as "Agenda FINAL".
   - Why: The Workshop creator and the super admin can remove a file, and a removal cannot be undone. Closing registrations and starting again is far too much, and the system cannot send files to attendees.
4. Try it before you look: your TRAINING workshop is published, has a poster and is in the Western Cape. A principal looks at the website Events page. What will she see?
   - Right answer: A card with no poster and a "Register" button that opens the booking page with the poster.
   - Why: The Events page never shows a workshop poster. There is no approval step, and bookings do not change the card. When the paid seats run out, the card shows "Full" with no link.
5. While you set up the workshop, a colleague asks for a 50 percent discount code for principals. Is this yours, or do you hand it on?
   - Right answer: Set up and publish the workshop, then pass the coupon request to Finance with the exact details.
   - Why: Discount codes are a Finance job. Only someone with the Finance tick (the setting that turns on money actions), an auditor or the super admin can make one. In the Western Cape that means the super admins, because nobody here has the Finance tick and there is no auditor. She does not have the Finance tick. Once made, Finance can change how many people may use a code, or switch it off and on, but never its price or the code itself. Halving the member price would change it for every member. Saying "the system cannot do discounts" is not true. She can say: "Discount codes are set up by our Finance person. I will pass that part to them today."
6. You click "Publish workshop" and the toast says "The workshop dates have passed. Review the dates or close registrations before sharing its link." The event has moved to next month. What do you do?
   - Right answer: Find it in "Needs closure", click "Edit details", set the new dates, "Save changes", then share.
   - Why: "Needs closure" is the group for published workshops whose dates have passed. Moving the dates into the future moves it back to "Current & upcoming" and Copy and Open work again. A new workshop would split the records, and dates are not a Finance job.
7. Your planning sheet says the venue is probably Parow Teachers' Centre, but the booking is only confirmed next week. What do you choose under "How it runs"?
   - Right answer: The dashed "To be confirmed" chip. Switch to "In person" with the venue in "Edit details" once it is confirmed.
   - Why: The booking page then says "Venue to be confirmed - we will email you as soon as it is arranged", which is true. Members read a typed venue as fact and plan their travel around it. Choosing "Online" would also be false.
8. You notice you created two drafts of the same workshop by mistake. Your manager says "just delete the extra one". What do you do?
   - Right answer: Select the extra draft and check its title. Click "Delete workshop", give the reason, tick "I understand this cannot be undone." and click "Delete workshop for good".
   - Why are you deleting it?: created by mistake
   - Why: Nobody has paid for a draft, so it can be deleted. The toast reads "“{workshop title}” deleted." Finance alone cannot delete a workshop. Publishing it just to close it would make it public on the Events page, even if only briefly. Once anyone has paid for a workshop, "Delete workshop" refuses and "Close registrations" is the only way.
9. Your workshop was today, from 09:00 to 15:00. At 17:00 a teacher phones. She found the booking link in an old WhatsApp message and has just booked. How is that possible, and what do you do?
   - Right answer: Registration only closes by itself at 00:05 tonight. Open "Published Links" > "Needs closure", select the workshop and click "Close registrations".
   - Why: The Events page dropped the workshop after 15:00, but the booking link stays open until someone clicks "Close registrations" or the nightly run closes it at 00:05. The page says "These workshop dates have passed but their registration links are still open." After closing, it moves to the archive and the link stops taking bookings. Had she phoned the next morning, the nightly run would already have closed it and the link would have refused her.

Cannot be undone: Nothing in this block.

If you are running late: Ask only the first question. The rest go into her workbook.

PART 3: BOOKING AND PAYING, FROM THE ATTENDEE'S SIDE (09:45 TO 10:40)

In this part she sits in the attendee's chair. She books her own training workshop the way a teacher or a school would, then looks at the same booking from the staff side. Members phone the office about what they see on the booking page, not about what staff see. Once she has booked herself, she can talk them through it. She can also tell straight away whether a booking is saved, paid or stuck.

One question runs through the whole part: is this mine to do, or do I hand it to Finance?

Every booking in this part goes on the workshop she created and published this morning:

- Title: TRAINING - Classroom Discipline - delete after
- Date and time: Saturday 14 Nov 2026, 08:30 to 12:30
- Venue: Parow Teachers' Centre
- Non-member price: R 450
- Member price: R 350
- Short code: TCDDA2
- Capacity: 60

No money moves today. Nobody pays for a TRAINING booking.

09:45 · Find the public booking link (2 min)

Goal: She now knows where the public booking link for a workshop lives, and that anyone with that link can book without logging in.

Who drives: Her screen.

Before you start:

- "TRAINING - Classroom Discipline - delete after" is published. She did that herself at 09:33.
- Her staff screen is on Workshops.

The Application link is the public address of the booking page. You share it with teachers and schools. The public page needs no login, so she opens it in a private (incognito) browser window and makes every booking there.

The link keeps taking bookings until someone clicks "Close registrations", or until the nightly run closes it at 00:05 after the workshop ends. The website's Events page drops the workshop by itself once its end time passes, but until that night's run anyone who still has the link or the QR code can book.

What happens:

1. She clicks Workshops, then "Published Links".
2. She selects "TRAINING - Classroom Discipline - delete after".
3. She copies its Application link.
4. She opens a private browser window and pastes the link.

She should see:

- The public booking page, on its first step "Confirm workshop details", with the title, date, place and price.

Cannot be undone: Nothing in this block.

If you are running late: Open the link on your second screen for her.

09:47 · She books as Sipho, then Sipho phones the office (12 min)

Goal: She learns that a booking exists the moment the person clicks "Save details and continue", but a seat only counts once the booking is paid. She now knows that marking an EFT paid is not possible for her. It is Finance's job. Her part is to pass on the EFT reference.

Who drives: Her screen. She makes the booking in the private window, then answers the call on her staff screen. You play the caller.

Timing: About 5 minutes for the booking, then about 7 minutes for the call.

Before you start:

- The private window shows the booking page for "TRAINING - Classroom Discipline - delete after".
- Her staff screen is on Workshops > "Published Links" with that workshop selected.
- Her own inbox, <Trainee work email>, is open in another tab. Every email from her bookings lands in her own inboxes.
- Your second screen has the same workshop open.
- Your facilitator card (hidden facts and success checks) and the hint cards are face down.
- Test cell number 1 from your seed sheet is to hand.

Ideas to explain as they come up:

- EFT is a bank transfer. When the person clicks "Send EFT instructions", the system emails them the bank details and an EFT reference.
- The EFT reference is a short code the payer must type exactly into their bank's reference box. It is built like EFT, then the short code TCDDA2, then a number, then up to 5 letters of the surname. It is the same on the success page, in the email and on the staff row. Finance uses it to match the bank statement.
- "Owes money" is the group in "All Registrations" where unpaid bookings sit. The same bookings show as "Bookings incomplete" in the workshop header. A seat is only counted once the booking is paid.
- The Attendee ledger is the list of every booking on this one workshop.
- The NAP number is the NAPTOSA membership number. The PERSAL number is a teacher's government staff number. It is not a NAPTOSA number.

First, the booking

What happens:

1. She reads the first step, "Confirm workshop details": the title, date, place and price box. The price box also shows "NAPTOSA members R ...".
2. She leaves "Has your school already paid for your place?" empty.
3. She clicks "Continue".
4. On "Who should be invoiced?" she clicks "Individual".
5. Under "Are you booking as a NAPTOSA member?" she clicks "No, standard rate R 450". The fee pill changes to "Standard fee".
6. She clicks "Continue".
7. On "Invoice and receipt details" she types Sipho's details:
   - Name: Sipho
   - Surname: Ndlovu
   - Email address: <Trainee work email>
   - Cell phone number: test cell number 1 from your seed sheet
   - PERSAL number (optional): leave it empty, or type the test PERSAL number from your seed sheet. Never a real one.
   - NAP number: not asked, because he is not a member
8. She clicks "Save details and continue".
9. On the payment step she reads "Invoice details are saved. Choose how you would like to pay."
10. She leaves the payment method on EFT and reads the note under it.
    - Payment method: EFT (it is the default)
    - Coupon code: none
11. She clicks "Send EFT instructions". She does not click "Card (demonstration)".
12. On the success page she reads the blue card "Pay R 450 by EFT", the line "Reference (use this exactly)" and "Payment status" "Awaiting EFT". She does not transfer any money. The bank details are for training only. Never pay them.
13. On her staff screen she clicks the Refresh button at the top of the Workshops page. She reads the workshop header and the "Bookings incomplete" box.
14. She expands "Registrations, coupons and documents" and finds the new row in the "Attendee ledger".
15. She clicks "Open this workshop's registration desk". This opens "All Registrations" for this workshop.
16. She opens the "Owes money" group and finds Sipho's row.
17. On that row she clicks "..." then "View details". She reads "Amount / method", "Payment reference", "Invoice" and "Email".
18. In the <Trainee work email> inbox she opens the email:
    - Subject: Workshop EFT payment instructions - TRAINING - Classroom Discipline - delete after

She should see:

- After saving the details: a green toast "Invoice and receipt details saved. Continue to payment."
- On the success page: "EFT instructions sent" above the heading "Workshop registration pending EFT", a reference like EFT-TCDDA2-NNN-NDLOV, "Payment status" "Awaiting EFT", "Email status" "Sent", and a "Pay by card instead" button she does not click. The NNN is a number the system picks.
- In the staff header after Refresh: "0 seats paid · 1 booking incomplete · 1 booking total". It is higher if other test bookings exist. "Bookings incomplete" goes up by one.
- In the Attendee ledger: a pill "EFT pending" and the same EFT reference. On a narrow screen the reference sits inside "Payment, receipt and email details". If a payment reminder is queued she also sees "Next reminder" and a date. The amount line with "Mark EFT paid" and "Cancel booking" only shows on Finance screens, so she does not see it.
- In "Owes money": the badge "Owes R ... · EFT" with R 450, the text "ref EFT-... · Not reminded yet", and "Attendance opens once the EFT is marked paid".
- In "View details": "Standard rate · EFT" under the amount, "Invoice" "Waits for payment", and "Email" "Sent · EFT instructions".
- The email shows the amount due, the EFT reference, the bank details and a "Pay by card instead" button.

Then, the phone call

Read her this before you start: "You work at the NAPTOSA Western Cape office. You are the Workshop creator. You do not have the Finance tick. A teacher who just booked a workshop is on the phone. Use the booking you made a few minutes ago to answer him, using the real staff screens. Try it before you look anything up in your notes."

The Finance tick is a permission on a staff account that unlocks the money buttons. She does not have it. In the Western Cape nobody has the Finance tick and there is no auditor, so Finance means the super admins. You are one today.

You play: Mr Sipho Ndlovu. He teaches Foundation Phase at Bellville South Primary and is not a NAPTOSA member. He booked on his phone, chose EFT and saw a page that said "pending". He has not paid. He is polite but a bit anxious about money and about losing his place. He talks quickly, in short sentences, and repeats his question when he is worried. A colleague has put ideas in his head.

Open with: "Hi, good morning. I booked that workshop on my phone just now, but I have not paid yet. Does my seat actually exist, or must I book again?"

Keep these to yourself until she asks:

- He has not checked his Spam or Junk folder. Say so only if she asks where he looked for the email.
- He plans to pay on Friday, when he gets paid. Say so only if she asks when he will pay.
- A colleague told him it would have been cheaper to say he is a member and type his PERSAL number in the member box. Say so only if she asks whether he has other questions, or mentions the member rate.
- He typed his email correctly. It is the address on the booking. Say so only if she asks him to confirm his email.

Stay inside these lines:

- He never gives a real ID, PERSAL, NAP or cell number. If asked, he says "it is on the booking", or you use the test values from your seed sheet.
- He never reads out or asks for card details, and never says he paid by card.
- No money moves. If she asks him to pay, he says "Friday".
- He stays a caller. He never coaches her, never names a button and never says whether she is right.

What happens:

Part one: does my booking exist?

19. You say (as Sipho): "Does my seat actually exist, or must I book again?"
20. She checks the staff side. Either she clicks Refresh and reads the "Bookings incomplete" count, or she finds his row under "Owes money" in "All Registrations".
21. She tells him the booking is saved but not paid. A seat only counts once it is paid. He must not book again.
22. If she freezes, you say: "Narrator: Your Published Links page is still showing what it showed before he booked." Saying "Narrator:" out loud tells her you have stepped out of character. This line points her to Refresh without naming it.
23. If she says "you have a seat", stay in character and say: "Great, so I don't have to pay?" Let her correct herself. He wants to hear he is "in".

Part two: the cheaper member price (optional. Play it only if time allows and the hidden fact has come out.)

24. You say (as Sipho): "My colleague says I should have said I am a member and put my PERSAL number in that box. Can you change me to the member price?"
25. She says no. The member rate is only for paid-up members. The number box wants a NAP number or an SA ID number, never a PERSAL number. His booking stays at the standard rate. If he wants details corrected, Finance handles it.
26. If she says "the system will catch you", you say in character: "Oh, so it checks?" See whether she corrects herself. It does not check. A good answer says the number is only recorded and NAPTOSA checks it later.

Part three: just mark it paid

27. If it helps, before this part you say, as a colleague: "So I just find the Mark EFT paid button, right?" She should notice she does not have that button.
28. You say (as Sipho): "Look, I will pay on Friday for sure. Can't you just mark it as paid now so I don't lose my place?"
29. She says she cannot mark an EFT paid. She names Finance (or the super admin) as the person who does it, once the money shows in NAPTOSA's bank account. She offers to pass on his EFT reference. She does not promise him a seat. Accept any wording close to: "Only our Finance person can mark it paid, once it shows in NAPTOSA's bank account. I will pass on your reference."
30. If you are skipping part four, end the call here and go straight to step 37.

Part four: the email never came (optional)

31. You say (as Sipho): "And the email with the bank details never came. Should I just book again so it sends a new one?"
32. She asks where he looked. He has not looked in Spam or Junk.
33. She tells him not to book again. Booking again would make a second booking and bring up a "Possible existing registration" warning.
34. She opens "View details" on his row and reads the "Email" line. It says "Sent · EFT instructions". That is her proof the email left.
35. She clicks "Preview eft instructions email" and reads him the amount and the reference. She reminds him to type the reference exactly. In training she only reads them out. Nobody pays.
36. If she clicks "Resend eft instructions email" instead, let her read the toast "EFT instructions email was already delivered, so it was not sent again." Then steer her to the preview.
37. You step out of character as coach. Name one thing she did well and ONE thing to improve. Only one.

She should see:

- Sipho's row under "Owes money", with the badge "Owes R ... · EFT".
- In "View details", the "Email" line "Sent · EFT instructions".
- After "Resend eft instructions email" on a sent email: the toast "EFT instructions email was already delivered, so it was not sent again."

If she gets stuck (give one hint at a time):

- Does his booking exist? "Before you answer, look at the booking with your own eyes on the staff side." Then: "Think about the difference between a booking that exists and a seat that is paid for."
- The member price: "What does the hint under the member number field say?" Then: "Is changing a booking after EFT instructions something the attendee or you can do?"
- Mark it paid: "Before you answer, decide whose job this is." Then: "What would Finance need from you to find his payment quickly?"
- The missing email: "Ask before you tell: where has he already looked?" Then: "Check what the booking's email status says on your side before you promise anything."

If it goes wrong:

- "Enter a South African cell (mobile) number, e.g. ..." with "Check the cell phone number above to continue.": a landline was typed. Use the test mobile number.
- "A PERSAL number is 5 to 10 digits. Check it, or leave it empty.": fix the number or clear the box.
- A warning "Possible existing workshop registration found." and a panel "Possible existing registration": that email, cell number or name already booked this workshop. Today every booking she makes uses her own email, so this is expected once her email is on an earlier booking. She reads the reason, for example "same email, same telephone", and clicks "Save as additional entry". In real life she only clicks it if it really is a second booking.
- The staff counters did not move: they do not update while you watch. Click the Refresh button.
- The success page says "Bank details needed" or "The bank details aren't ready on this page yet...": Settings > Payment details is not saved for the province. Stop and tell the super admin.
- The email has not arrived: check Spam or Junk first. Then open "View details" and read "Email". If it says "Sent", "Resend eft instructions email" will not send it again. Use "Preview eft instructions email" to read the amount and reference to the person instead. Only if it says "Failed" does "Resend eft instructions email" try again.
- A red banner "This workshop has reached capacity. New registrations are closed.": the workshop's paid seats are used up. Raise the capacity.

Not possible, and what to say instead:

- "Can I pay by card instead, with my real card?" Real card payments are not available on ordinary workshops. PayFast only works on two pilot workshops. "Card (demonstration)" moves no money and is not used today. Today is EFT only, and never a real card. In real life, tell the member to pay by EFT using the reference exactly as shown.
- "Can I pay half now and half next month?" There are no partial payments. A booking is paid in full or not at all, and Finance only marks it paid for the full amount. The booking stays under "Owes money" until then, and payment reminders go out while it is unpaid.
- "Please mark my EFT paid now." "Mark EFT paid" is a Finance action. Only someone with the Finance tick, an auditor or the super admin can do it. In the Western Cape nobody has the Finance tick and there is no auditor, so that means the super admins. A Workshop creator does not see the button, and the system would refuse her anyway. She says: "Only our Finance person can mark it paid, once it shows on NAPTOSA's bank statement. I will pass your reference to Finance." Then she gives Finance the EFT reference.
- "Have you checked that I am a member?" or "Just put me in as a member." There is no membership lookup during booking. She says the member rate is for paid-up members and the number is recorded for NAPTOSA to check. A non-member books at the standard rate. A PERSAL number only goes in the optional PERSAL box, never in the NAP box.

Cannot be undone: The EFT instructions email to <Trainee work email> cannot be recalled. Once "Send EFT instructions" is clicked the booking is frozen. That means nobody can change it and only Finance can cancel it. The booking also creates a new contact in CRM contacts with the source "Workshop". That is normal, not an error, and she never edits those contacts from the workshop screens.

If you are running late: Never drop this block. Skip the member price part and the missing email part of the call.

Debrief:

1. "What did you look at on the staff side before you told him his booking existed, and what exactly did it say?"
2. "Say it in your own words: whose job is 'Mark EFT paid', and what do you do instead?"
3. "Do you really know why the member trick does not work, or does it just feel like you know? Explain it without your notes."

How did she do?

- Confirms the booking from the staff side. Done well when she pressed Refresh or opened "Owes money", found Sipho, and said "saved, not paid, the seat counts once paid". Partly when she said the booking exists without checking, or checked but told him he "has a seat". Missed when she told him to book again or guessed without looking.
- Handles the member price temptation truthfully (optional part). Done well when she refused, explained NAP or ID number (not PERSAL), and did not claim the system checks membership. Partly when she refused but said "the system will check", or could not say what the field wants. Missed when she agreed to change him to the member rate or suggested he book again as a member.
- Hands "Mark EFT paid" to Finance. Done well when she said she cannot do it, named Finance or the super admin, and offered to pass on the EFT reference. Partly when she said she cannot do it but not who can, or promised a seat. Missed when she tried to mark it paid or told him it is "done".
- Solves the missing email without confusion (optional part). Done well when she asked about Spam or Junk, checked the "Email" line in "View details", told him not to book again, and used "Preview eft instructions email" to give him the amount and reference. Partly when she checked the status but promised a resend the system will not send, or did not tell him to avoid booking again. Missed when she told him to book again or invented a different fix.

09:59 · She books as a member at the member price (6 min)

Goal: She learns that the member price is self-declared. The NAP number is only written down for NAPTOSA to check later. She now knows that the system does not look up membership, and that the only check is the check digit on a 13-digit SA ID number.

Who drives: Her screen. She books in the private window, then looks at the staff side.

Before you start:

- The private window is open on the Application link for "TRAINING - Classroom Discipline - delete after".
- From your seed sheet: test member NAP number 01, test cell number 2, and the synthetic invalid ID.
- She books with her own personal email, <Trainee personal email>, and a fresh test cell number. If her email is already on an earlier booking for this workshop, the duplicate check speaks up. That is expected. See "If it goes wrong".

Ideas to explain as they come up:

- The NAP number is the NAPTOSA membership number. It is printed on a teacher's payslip next to the NAPTOSA deduction. It is not the PERSAL number, which is the teacher's government staff number.
- The check digit is the last digit of a 13-digit SA ID number. It is worked out from the other 12 digits, so a typo usually breaks it.
- The duplicate check warns with "Possible existing registration" when the same email, cell number or name has already booked this workshop. The panel names the reason, for example "same email, same telephone". Today she reuses her own two emails on purpose, so she sees it later in this part and clicks "Save as additional entry".
- The price is locked when the booking is saved. A later price change on the workshop never changes what this booking owes.
- The EFT reference is the code the payer types into their bank's reference box. Finance uses it to match the bank statement.
- "Owes money" is the group in "All Registrations" where unpaid bookings sit.

What happens:

1. She opens the Application link. On "Confirm workshop details" she clicks "Continue".
2. On "Who should be invoiced?" she clicks "Individual".
3. She clicks "Yes, member rate R 350".
4. In the member number box she types the invalid ID and reads the message.
   - NAP number (preferred) or SA ID number: the synthetic invalid ID from your seed sheet
5. She deletes it and types the test NAP number instead.
   - NAP number (preferred) or SA ID number: test member NAP number 01 from your seed sheet
6. She reads the hint under the box out loud: "Your NAP number is printed on your payslip, next to the NAPTOSA deduction. This is not your PERSAL number."
7. She clicks "Continue".
8. On "Invoice and receipt details" she types Refilwe's details:
   - Name: Refilwe
   - Surname: Moloi
   - Email address: <Trainee personal email>
   - Cell phone number: test cell number 2 from your seed sheet
   - PERSAL number: not asked. A member is not asked for PERSAL.
9. She clicks "Save details and continue".
10. On the payment step she leaves the method on EFT, with no coupon.
    - Payment method: EFT
    - Coupon code: none
11. She clicks "Send EFT instructions".
12. On her staff screen she opens "All Registrations".
13. In the "Workshop" switcher she chooses "TRAINING - Classroom Discipline - delete after".
14. She opens the "Owes money" group.
15. On Refilwe's row she clicks "..." then "View details".

She should see:

- After the invalid ID: "That looks like an SA ID number but the check digit does not match. Check the digits, or use your NAP membership number instead."
- After the NAP number: "NAPTOSA uses this to match your booking to your membership." The fee pill reads "Member fee".
- On the success page: "Amount due (member rate)" with R 350, an EFT reference ending in MOLOI, and "Payment status" "Awaiting EFT".
- On the staff row: the badge "Owes R ... · EFT" with R 350.
- In "View details": "Member rate · EFT" under the amount, and "ID / member no" showing exactly what she typed.
- Nothing anywhere says the membership was checked, because it was not.

If it goes wrong:

- A red "Choose one of these to continue." and "Tell us whether you are booking as a NAPTOSA member.": answer the member question first.
- "Required to book at the member rate. Either number is fine." with "Enter your NAP number or SA ID number.": the number box is empty.
- A PERSAL number typed into the NAP box is accepted without any warning, because NAP numbers have no format check. Read the hint again and correct it before saving.
- A "Possible existing registration" panel: her email or cell number is already on a booking for this workshop. She reads the reason, for example "same email, same telephone", and clicks "Save as additional entry".

Not possible, and what to say instead:

- A member asks "You have checked that I am a member, right?", or a non-member asks you to "just put me in as a member". There is no membership lookup during booking. The NAP or ID number is only recorded for NAPTOSA to check later. It never changes or confirms the price. She says plainly that the member rate is for paid-up members and the number is recorded for NAPTOSA to check. A non-member books at the standard rate.

Cannot be undone: The EFT instructions email to <Trainee personal email> cannot be recalled. The booking is frozen, which means nobody can change it and only Finance can cancel it. A new contact is created in CRM contacts with the source "Workshop".

If you are running late: Skip the whole block. It is number 4 on your drop-first list. Say in one sentence that the member rate is self-declared and the number is only recorded.

10:05 · Coupons: Finance makes them, she uses them (5 min)

Goal: She realises that creating a coupon is not possible for her. It is Finance's job. She learns that a coupon changes the amount on the payment step, that a members-only code is refused for a non-member, and that only Finance can change a coupon afterwards. Finance can change the number of people and the note, or switch it off. The code and the price never change.

Who drives: Both. You create the coupons on your Finance screen (your second screen). She looks at the coupons on her own screen and applies them in the private window.

Before you start:

- Your second screen is signed in as super admin.
- Her staff screen is on Workshops > "Published Links" with "TRAINING - Classroom Discipline - delete after" selected.
- From your seed sheet: test cell number 3. For the optional free booking, also test cell number 4 and test member NAP number 02.

Ideas to explain as they come up:

- A coupon is a code Finance creates on one workshop. It either makes the booking free (the "Comp" option) or sets one fixed price for everyone who uses it. It can be for all bookings or for members only. A use is only counted once the booking is paid.
- Finance here means a super admin, an auditor or any staff account with the Finance tick, which is the permission that unlocks the money buttons. In the Western Cape nobody has the Finance tick and there is no auditor, so Finance means the super admins. You are one today. She is not.
- EFT is a bank transfer. "Send EFT instructions" emails the person the bank details and a reference.
- "Owes money" is the group in "All Registrations" for unpaid bookings. "Check in" is the group for paid bookings.

What happens:

1. You (as Finance) go to "Published Links" and select "TRAINING - Classroom Discipline - delete after".
2. You open "Registrations, coupons and documents", then "Coupons".
3. You fill the "Create coupon" form for the first coupon and click "Create coupon":
   - Applies to: All bookings
   - Code: TRAINFIX
   - What it does: Fixed price
   - Price in ZAR: 200
   - How many people: 3
   - Why (optional): training
4. You fill the form again for the second coupon and click "Create coupon":
   - Applies to: Members only
   - Code: TRAINFREE
   - What it does: Comp (free)
   - How many people: 2
5. She opens the same "Coupons" panel on her own screen and reads what a non-Finance user sees.
6. If the new coupons are not listed on her screen, she clicks another workshop and then back, or reloads the page. The Refresh button does not reload the Coupons list.
7. In the private window she opens the Application link and clicks "Continue".
8. On "Who should be invoiced?" she clicks "Individual".
9. She clicks "No, standard rate R 450", then "Continue".
10. On "Invoice and receipt details" she types Carmen's details:
    - Name: Carmen
    - Surname: Februarie
    - Email address: <Trainee work email>
    - Cell phone number: test cell number 3 from your seed sheet
11. She clicks "Save details and continue". This comes before the coupon, because saving the details again clears a coupon. Her work email is already on Sipho's booking, so the panel "Possible existing registration" opens. She reads the reason, for example "same email, same telephone", and clicks "Save as additional entry".
12. On the payment step she types the members-only code in the "Enter coupon code" box and clicks "Apply". She reads the message.
    - Coupon code: TRAINFREE
13. She types the fixed-price code and clicks "Apply".
    - Coupon code: TRAINFIX
14. She chooses EFT and clicks "Send EFT instructions". Applying a code, or pressing Enter in the box, never pays by itself.
    - Payment method: EFT
15. Optional, once only: she opens the Application link again, clicks "Continue", then "Individual", then "Yes, member rate R 350".
16. She types the member number and clicks "Continue".
    - NAP number (preferred) or SA ID number: test member NAP number 02 from your seed sheet
17. On "Invoice and receipt details" she types Bongani's details and clicks "Save details and continue":
    - Name: Bongani
    - Surname: Mabaso
    - Email address: <Trainee work email>
    - Cell phone number: test cell number 4 from your seed sheet

    Her work email is already on Sipho's and Carmen's bookings. Bongani never uses her personal email, because Anita needs it for her school place at 10:10. When "Possible existing registration" opens, she reads the reason and clicks "Save as additional entry".
18. On the payment step she applies the free code and clicks "Confirm free booking". There is no payment method to choose, because the coupon covers the full fee.
    - Coupon code: TRAINFREE
19. Back on her staff screen she clicks another workshop and back, then reads the Coupons list again.
20. Optional: she watches your Finance screen. On the TRAINFIX row you click "Edit", read the box, type the new values and click "Save":
    - How many people: 4
    - Why (optional): training, one extra place
21. Optional: on the same row you click "Switch off", read the box and click "Switch off". Carmen's unpaid booking still holds the code, so the system refuses and the coupon stays on.
22. She checks her own Coupons list. She sees the coupons but no "Edit" or "Switch off" buttons.

She should see:

- On her screen: the coupons list and, where the form would be, "Only a super admin, an auditor or a Finance user can create coupons."
- On your Finance screen after each one: the toast "Coupon created."
- TRAINFREE on Carmen, a non-member: the red toast "This coupon can only be used by NAPTOSA members."
- TRAINFIX: the toast "Coupon applied. Amount due is now R ..." and the green line "Amount due: R ...", both showing R 200.
- Only if Bongani's free booking was made: the toast "Coupon applied. This booking is fully covered.", the payment method section disappears, and a green box says the coupon covers the full fee. After "Confirm free booking": "Workshop registration confirmed" and an email "Workshop invoice and receipt - TRAINING - Classroom Discipline - delete after" showing R 0.00 and the method Coupon.
- In the Coupons list: TRAINFIX still shows all its uses left, because a pending EFT uses none yet. If the free booking was made, TRAINFREE shows one use fewer, because that booking is already paid.
- In "All Registrations": Carmen's booking under "Owes money" at the coupon price. If the free booking was made, it sits under "Check in" with "Paid R 0,00 · Coupon (no charge)".
- Only if the optional Edit was shown: the box "Edit coupon TRAINFIX" with "0 of 3 used. To change the price or the code, switch this coupon off and create a new one.", then the toast "Coupon TRAINFIX saved. N of 4 left."
- Only if the optional Switch off was shown: the box "Switch off coupon TRAINFIX?" with "Nobody can use this code from now on. People who already used it keep their place.", then the refusal "1 unpaid booking is using this code. Cancel that booking, or wait until it is paid, then switch the coupon off."
- On her own screen: the coupons, with no "Edit", "Switch off" or "Switch on" buttons.

If it goes wrong:

- "That code isn't valid for this workshop.": the same message shows for a wrong, used-up, switched-off or other-workshop code. Check the code against this workshop's Coupons list with Finance.
- The coupon disappeared after clicking "Save details and continue" again: saving the details clears the coupon. Apply the code last, on the payment step.
- "EFT instructions have already been issued. Contact staff to change this booking.": the booking is frozen and a coupon cannot be added any more. Hand it to Finance.
- Finance gets "That code already exists on this workshop. Choose a different code.": choose another code.
- A "Possible existing registration" panel: her own email is already on a booking for this workshop. That is expected today. She reads the reason, for example "same email, same telephone", and clicks "Save as additional entry".
- Finance gets "How many people may use it? A whole number above zero." when saving an edit: type a whole number above zero. The number also cannot go below the people who used the code plus the people holding it.
- Finance gets "Someone changed this coupon a moment ago. The list has been refreshed. Check it and try again.": someone else saved first. Check the list and try again.
- She looks for a "Create coupon" form on her screen and only finds "Only a super admin, an auditor or a Finance user can create coupons.": that is correct. It is a Finance job.

Not possible, and what to say instead:

- "Finance, please fix the coupon you made: wrong price, switch it off, or change the number of uses." Finance can change the number of people and the note with "Edit", and can stop the code with "Switch off" and bring it back with "Switch on". The code, the kind, the price and "Applies to" never change. For a wrong price, Finance switches the coupon off and creates a new one with a different code. Finance cannot switch a coupon off while an unpaid booking holds the code. That booking must be paid or cancelled first. People who already used a switched-off code keep their place. She sees the coupons but has no "Edit" or "Switch off" buttons, so she stops sharing the wrong code and tells Finance straight away. In the Western Cape, Finance means the super admins.
- "I got my EFT instructions. Now I want to change my name or headcount, add a coupon, or cancel it myself." Once EFT instructions are sent, the booking is frozen. The attendee sees "EFT instructions have already been issued. Contact staff to change this booking." The attendee cannot cancel or edit it, and staff cannot edit those fields either. She hands it to Finance. Finance can use "Correct payer email" for a wrong email. For anything else, Finance cancels the unpaid booking with "Cancel booking" and the person books again.
- "I pressed Refresh but the new coupon is not in the Coupons list." The Refresh button at the top reloads the workshop list, not the Coupons list. Click another workshop and then back, or reload the page.

Cannot be undone: Both coupons stay on the workshop. Their code and price can never change. Add TRAINFIX and TRAINFREE to the super admin clean-up list, to switch off once no unpaid booking holds them. TRAINFIX stays on while Carmen's booking is unpaid. Carmen's EFT instructions email cannot be recalled. Bongani's free booking, if made, becomes a paid booking with a receipt and uses one coupon place.

If you are running late: Skip the whole block, or skip only Bongani's free booking and the optional Edit and Switch off. This block is number 2 on your drop-first list.

10:10 · The school clerk who wants one invoice for three teachers (13 min)

Goal: She learns that a school can pay once for several teachers, and that no teacher ID numbers are needed at booking. She realises that the school code only exists after Finance marks the payment paid, and that marking it paid is not possible for her. She now knows a member may take a non-member place, but a non-member can never take a member place.

Who drives: Both. She books on the public page while you, as the clerk, give her the details. You also press the Finance button on your second screen. Later she registers one teacher.

Before you start:

- The private window is open on the Application link for "TRAINING - Classroom Discipline - delete after".
- Her staff screen is on "All Registrations" with that workshop chosen in the "Workshop" switcher.
- You are signed in as super admin on "All Registrations" on your second screen.
- Her two inboxes, <Trainee work email> and <Trainee personal email>, are open in one mail client. The school's email is her work email. Anita's email is her personal email. It must be: a teacher on a school place is refused if the email already has a paid booking on the workshop ("This email already has a paid booking for this workshop."), and her work email already holds the paid school booking.
- The synthetic valid ID from your seed sheet is on your facilitator card only, never on her handouts.
- The workshop's "Capacity" box shows at least 3 seats still free. It counts paid seats against the limit, and the school's headcount counts against capacity.
- Your facilitator card (hidden facts and success checks) and the hint cards are face down.
- Bergrivier is booked once, marked paid once and Anita registers once. Do not repeat any of them.

Ideas to explain as they come up:

- A school booking pays once for a number of member places and non-member places. The school is not asked for any ID or NAP number.
- The Finance tick is a permission on a staff account that unlocks the money buttons, such as "Mark EFT paid". She does not have it. In the Western Cape nobody has the Finance tick and there is no auditor, so Finance means the super admins. You are one today.
- The EFT reference is the code the payer types into their bank's reference box. Finance uses it to find the payment on the bank statement.
- "Owes money" is the group in "All Registrations" for unpaid bookings. "Check in" is the group for paid bookings. "Done" is where the paid school row moves to. Its teachers then check in on their own places.
- The school code is printed in the school's receipt email. The school only gets it, with a "Register a teacher" link, after Finance marks the payment paid. Teachers use it to register themselves at R 0,00.
- INV and RCT numbers are the invoice and receipt numbers the system issues when Finance marks a booking paid. They are real numbers that count in "Revenue collected", so you log each one on the training receipts list.

Read her this before you start: "A school clerk is on the phone. Her principal wants to send three teachers and pay once. You will fill in the public booking page while she gives you the details, using your own work email as the school's email. You are the Workshop creator without the Finance tick. Keep asking yourself: is this mine to do, or do I hand it to Finance?"

You play: Mrs Elmarie Pienaar, admin clerk at Bergrivier Primary School in Wellington. She is efficient, friendly and busy, and used to doing things for the whole staffroom. She speaks in practical questions and wants things done in one go. Her principal asked her to book three teachers, two NAPTOSA members and one non-member, on one invoice for the school. She does not have the teachers' ID numbers with her. Later she wants to share the code as widely as possible.

Open with: "Morning! The principal wants to send three teachers to your workshop. Two are NAPTOSA members, one is not. Can the school get one invoice for all of them?"

Keep these to yourself until she asks:

- The school wants to pay by EFT. Say so if she asks how the school will pay.
- The school has no VAT number. Say so only if she asks about VAT.
- She plans to put the school code in the staffroom WhatsApp group, with about 40 teachers. Say so when she gets the code, or if asked who she will send it to.
- Only Anita (a non-member) and two member teachers are meant to go. Johan, another non-member, also wants to attend but was not in the headcount. Say so only if she asks whether the three names are final.

Stay inside these lines:

- She never gives a real ID, PERSAL, NAP or cell number. The teacher ID used on screen is the synthetic valid ID from your seed sheet.
- She never offers card details. The school pays by EFT.
- She never coaches. She does not name buttons or say whether an answer is right.
- She sticks to the headcount: 2 members, 1 non-member.

What happens:

Part one: one invoice, no ID numbers

1. You say (as Elmarie): "Can the school get one invoice? And do you need the teachers' ID numbers now? I don't have them here."
2. She opens the Application link and clicks "Continue".
3. On "Who should be invoiced?" she clicks "School".
4. Under "How many people are attending?" she types the headcount:
   - Members · R ... each: 2
   - Non-members · R ... each: 1
5. She reads "Total people" and "Total price" back to you.
6. She tells you no teacher ID numbers are needed now. Each teacher gives their own details later.
7. She clicks "Continue".
8. She asks you for the school's details. If she hunts for an ID field for the school, stay in character: "So what do you need from me?"
9. She types the school's details:
   - School name: Bergrivier Primary School
   - Address: a training address, for example "TRAINING ONLY - no event" on line 1 and the suburb on line 2
   - VAT number: leave it empty
   - Email address: <Trainee work email>
10. She clicks "Save details and continue". If the panel "Possible existing registration" opens because her work email is already on earlier bookings, she reads the reason and clicks "Save as additional entry".
11. On the payment step she reads the "Amount due" box for 3 people.
12. She chooses EFT and clicks "Send EFT instructions".
    - Payment method: EFT
    - Coupon code: none

Part two: we paid, please confirm

13. You say (as Elmarie): "Great, I've done the EFT from the school account just now. Can you confirm it so the teachers can register today?"
14. On her staff screen she opens "All Registrations", the "Owes money" group, then the Bergrivier row.
15. She looks for a "Mark EFT paid" button and does not find one.
16. She tells you Finance marks it paid once it shows on NAPTOSA's bank statement. The school code and teacher link come in the receipt email after that.
17. She passes it to Finance (you) with the EFT reference.
18. Only after she has handed it over, you switch hats out loud: "Narrator: Finance has found the payment on the statement." Saying "Narrator:" tells her you have stepped out of character.
19. You (as Finance, on your second screen) open "All Registrations", then "Owes money", then the Bergrivier row, and click "Mark EFT paid".
20. You read the dialog, then click "Mark paid and send receipt". Do this once only.
21. She reads the toast and sees the row move to "Check in".
22. She opens the <Trainee work email> inbox and the receipt email. She finds the "School code:" line and the "Register a teacher" link.
    - Subject: Workshop invoice and receipt - TRAINING - Classroom Discipline - delete after

Part three: can I put the code on WhatsApp?

23. You say (as Elmarie): "We got the receipt with a school code! Can I just put it in the staffroom WhatsApp group so everyone can register?"
24. She tells you the code covers exactly 3 places, 2 member and 1 non-member. It must only go to the three named teachers, because once the places are used nobody else can register on it.
25. As Anita, she opens the "Register a teacher" link and clicks "No, I am not a member".
26. She types Anita's details:
    - First name: Anita
    - Surname: Fourie
    - Email: <Trainee personal email>
    - SA ID number: the synthetic valid ID from your seed sheet
    - Cell number (optional): leave it empty
    - PERSAL number (optional): leave it empty
27. She clicks "Register my place (R 0,00)". There is no payment step.
28. On her staff screen she opens "Check in" and finds Anita's row.
29. She clicks the underlined school name and reads the teachers sheet.
30. If you are skipping part four, end the call here. Step out of character as coach: name one thing she did well and ONE thing to improve.

Part four: Johan cannot get in (optional)

31. You say (as Elmarie): "Johan says Bergrivier paid for him too, but the page tells him the only places left are member places. He is not a member. Can you sort it out?"
32. As Johan, she opens the same "Register a teacher" link and clicks "No, I am not a member". She types nothing else. She reads the message.
33. She explains that a member may take a non-member place but never the reverse. She cannot swap places. The school must contact NAPTOSA so Finance can sort it out with the school.
34. She points to "Places used 1 of 3" on the teachers sheet.
35. If she asks whether the three names were final, tell her Johan was never in the headcount. Do not let her suggest Johan "just ticks member".
36. Step out of character as coach: name one thing she did well and ONE thing to improve.

She should see:

- The fee pill "School total R ... · 3 people". On the success page, "Attendees" "3 people: 2 members, 1 non-member" and an EFT reference ending in BERGR.
- On the staff row before payment: "Owes R ... · EFT" and "School · 2 members, 1 non-member (3 total)". No "Mark EFT paid" button on her screen.
- In your Finance dialog: "Mark R ... paid for Bergrivier Primary School (2 members, 1 non-member (3 total))?" and "Check the bank statement shows reference EFT-... A receipt is emailed immediately and this cannot be undone."
- The toast, in the form "payer in workshop, ref EFT reference: EFT marked paid and the receipt was emailed." The row moves to "Check in" with "Paid R ... · EFT".
- In the school's receipt email: a box "Your teachers register themselves", a "School code:" line with the code, a "Register a teacher" button, and a PDF "COMBINED INVOICE & RECEIPT" with Members and Non-members lines.
- On Anita's page before registering: "Paid for by Bergrivier Primary School. This covers your place.", "2 member and 1 non-member places left (0 of 3 used)." and "Amount due: R 0,00. There is no payment step."
- After registering: "Registered on a school place" and "You're registered, Anita". The email "You are registered - TRAINING - Classroom Discipline - delete after" goes to Anita, and "Anita Fourie registered - 1 of 3 places used - TRAINING - Classroom Discipline - delete after" goes to the school.
- On the staff side: Anita's row shows "Paid by school", "School place · R 0,00" and "Place paid for by Bergrivier Primary School · 1 of 3 places used". The school row is now in "Done" with "Teachers check in on their own places · 1 registered". The sheet "Teachers on Bergrivier Primary School's places" shows "Places used 1 of 3" and "Paid: 2 member, 1 non-member · 2 unclaimed places".
- Only if the Johan part is played: Johan sees straight away "The places still open are member places. A non-member cannot take a member place. Ask Bergrivier Primary School to contact NAPTOSA." No form opens and no row is created for him.

If she gets stuck (give one hint at a time):

- One invoice: "Look at what the page asks a school for, and what it does not ask." Then: "Read the total back to her before you save."
- We paid: "Check your own screen before you promise anything." Then: "What will Finance need from you to find this payment?"
- WhatsApp: "How many places did the school pay for, and of which kind?" Then: "What happens to the fourth person who uses the code?"
- Johan: "Read the screen Johan sees before you answer." Then: "Is there anything on your side that changes a place? If not, who deals with the school?"

If it goes wrong:

- "Enter at least one attendee to continue.": both boxes are 0.
- "Only N seat(s) are left for this workshop." when saving: the school's headcount is more than the seats left. Raise the capacity.
- "Complete the school billing details.": a school field is empty.
- You (as Finance) see "This workshop is full. Refund this person, then use Cancel booking on this row." instead of the button: the workshop is full. Stop. Do not mark it paid.
- A toast "...EFT marked paid. The receipt email did not send...": you (as Finance) open the row's "..." menu in "Check in" and choose "Resend receipt email".
